- 00:20 IST · **[BLUE BEE] Corporate accounts + per-account rate cards + building-match pricing (members pay individually) — branch `feat/corporate-pricing` off `main` (`b6ec120`); feature `5150c12` (+ this bookkeeping commit); single CC session. Pushed to `origin/feat/corporate-pricing`. NOT merged — founder lands it. Additive + reversible migration (14 → 15); `main` `b6ec120` + the foundation `7d1fa47` untouched; no force-push.** The platform was **already B2B-priced** (`PricingService.resolveRate`: account_override → account list → org-default list → `Service.base_rate`). This increment adds the **owner/web management** (corporate account + buildings + a per-pound/per-item rate card + a join code) and the **engine** that prices a resident at a corporate building — or a join-code member — on the corporate rate card **while they still pay individually**. `resolveRate` is **REUSED unchanged** — this changes *which account* prices an order, not *how* a rate resolves. **DEFERRED (modeled-for, NOT faked):** the customer-app join-code **entry screen** + the "you're on [Building] pricing" display (the next increment — makes the TTC path end-to-end; the redeem endpoint + memberships read ship + are e2e-tested now); visual dry-clean catalog with item images; in-app chat; service-area geo-blocking/drawn zones; consolidated (property-manager-pays) billing (already exists via `BATCH_PERIODIC`, untouched); email-domain enrollment. **Key decisions:** (1) **effective pricing account = a nullable `Order.pricingAccountId`** (FK → Account, `ON DELETE SET NULL`) — `Order.accountId` stays the **payer** (the member's own account → PER_ORDER, RBAC scope, order list, batch billing all UNCHANGED); the quote engine resolves on `order.pricingAccountId ?? order.accountId`. **Decouples WHO IS PRICED from WHO PAYS** (chosen over re-parenting the order under the corporate account, which would corrupt RBAC scope + the order list + batch billing). Set only when it differs from the payer (guarded), else NULL ⇒ default/residential pricing. (2) **building-match boundary v1 = address point + radius** — a `Site` with `geo` (PostGIS `geography(Point,4326)`) AND `match_radius_m` (metres, [10, 5000]) is a corporate building; match = real `ST_DWithin(geo, point, radius)` (geography metres), ties broken by nearest centre (`ST_Distance`, `LIMIT 1`) → deterministic; postal/explicit-address match documented as a later option. (3) **membership is a dedicated `CorporateMembership` model, NOT a `UserRole`** — deliberately **pricing-only**: it never widens order read/write scope (a join-code member can never see another member's orders nor book under the corporate account); member reads (`GET /v1/corporate/memberships`) are **userId-scoped** (the Message/Notification pattern). (4) **precedence at booking:** building match → membership → none (the physical address is the most specific, member-independent signal). (5) the booking gains an **optional `serviceGeo`** on `bookOrderSchema` (backward-compatible — existing callers + the unchanged customer app unaffected). **Migration (`20260630120000_corporate_pricing_inc15`, additive only):** `Account` +`corporate`/`join_code`(unique)/`join_code_expires_at`; `Order` +`pricing_account_id`(FK SET NULL)+index; `Site` +`match_radius_m`; new `CorporateMembership` (unique (userId, accountId); FKs CASCADE) + 2 enums. **A `migrate diff` also emits two `DROP INDEX` for `ParLevel_/LinenStock_site_cost_center_service_key` — DELIBERATELY OMITTED** (migration-14's raw `NULLS NOT DISTINCT` unique indexes the Prisma `@@unique` can't express → a pre-existing phantom drift; the linen `ON CONFLICT` upsert depends on them). **API (`apps/api/src/corporate/*` + `orders/*`):** `CorporateController` (`/v1/corporate`) — management **OWNER/FINANCE only** (accounts CRUD · buildings add/list/patch · `GET rate-card` showing each service's account rate · default rate · base · **resolved effective rate + source** · `PUT rate-card/items/:serviceId` · `POST join-code`); member surface (any authenticated user) — **`POST /redeem`** (idempotent; bad/expired → 400) + **`GET /memberships`** (userId-scoped). New `PricingAccountService` (orders module) — `resolveForBooking` does the real-PostGIS building match + membership lookup; wired into `OrdersService.book` (sets `pricing_account_id` + prices the estimate on the effective account) and `QuoteService.build` (the quote resolves on the effective account). Rate card writes the **account-bound `PriceList`/`PriceListItem`** (real §9.2 rows, lazily created). **Web (`apps/web`, Daylight, Next 14 / React 18.2):** **`/corporate`** (directory + create) + **`/corporate/[id]`** (join-code card · buildings card with address+lat/lng+radius · **rate-card editor** with the resolved effective rate + source badge) — nav under **Commercial**, `can.corporate` = OWNER/FINANCE (mirrors the server `@Roles`); `lib/api-client.ts` corporate methods. **Seed/demo:** **Maple District Towers** (corporate, PER_ORDER) — 1 building (200 Bay St, 300 m), an **LB $1.80 + ITEM $9.00** rate card (vs $2.50/$12.00 retail), join code **`MAPLELOFT`**, and an **in-building resident** (Tom Becker) with a DELIVERED order priced corporate ($36 = 20 lb × $1.80) while paying individually. **6-lens adversarial QC (refute-by-default):** tenancy (corp-A member never sees/gets corp-B data/pricing; cross-account/cross-org → 403/404) · pricing correctness (deterministic match; LB **and** ITEM resolve corporate; no-match → default) · payment (the member pays PER_ORDER; the corporate account is never charged) · no §5.1 corruption · RBAC (only OWNER/FINANCE manage; a member can't edit pricing) · no-stub/geo (real PostGIS, real PriceList rows, real `UserRole`-free membership) — all clean + e2e-locked. **Evidence (full stack live — Postgres+Redis+MinIO):** api **typecheck** clean · **unit 221/221** (28 suites, incl. NEW `pricing-account.service` precedence spec 5/5) · **NEW `corporate-pricing` e2e 16/16** (management + RBAC 403 / cross-org 404 / non-corporate 400; building match LB **and** ITEM priced corporate + **pays individually, $0 on the corp account**; outside → default; redeem idempotent + bad/expired → 400; **tenancy: memberships userId-scoped · corp-A member never gets corp-B's $5 rate · cross-org code invalid**; residential no-regression) · **full e2e 264/264, 25 suites** (a prior run's 21 "failures" were ALL `MinIO not reachable` env-only — gone with `infra:up`) · **fresh-DB migrate 14 → 15 + seed → clean** · **`/v1/health` 200** (postgis 3.4.3 / timescaledb 2.26.4 / redis PONG) · web **typecheck + `next lint`** clean · **component tests 122/122** (25 suites, incl. NEW `CorporateAccounts.test` 5/5) · **`next build` green** (routes `/corporate` + `/corporate/[id]`). **Next increment:** wire the customer-app join-code entry + corporate-pricing display against the shipped `POST /v1/corporate/redeem` + `GET /v1/corporate/memberships`. · → apps/api/src/{corporate/* (new), orders/{pricing-account.service.ts (new), pricing-account.service.spec.ts (new), orders.service.ts, quote.service.ts, orders.module.ts}, app.module.ts}, apps/api/{prisma/{schema.prisma, seed.ts, migrations/20260630120000_corporate_pricing_inc15/* (new)}, test/corporate-pricing.e2e-spec.ts (new)}, apps/web/{app/(console)/corporate/* (new), components/{CorporateAccounts.tsx, CorporateAccounts.test.tsx (new), AppShell.tsx}, lib/{api-client,rbac}.ts}, packages/shared/src/{corporate.ts (new), orders.ts, index.ts}, handoffs/2026-07-01-0020-handoff.md

- 02:07 IST · **[BLUE BEE] Competitive teardown — Poplin (formerly SudShare) analysed end-to-end & benchmarked vs Blue Bee (research/strategy deliverable, NOT a code build; no repo code touched).** Triggered by a customer-supplied reference (`play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.sudshare.sudshare` + `poplin.co`). Senior-analyst brief synthesising CEO/CMO/GTM lenses; answers the six asks (GTM · target market · earnings · onboarding · customers · how the app works) + funding/investment + the head-to-head vs Blue Bee. **Findings:** Poplin = SudShare rebrand (May 2023); two-sided **consumer gig marketplace** ("Uber for laundry"), **wash-dry-fold only**, pros use their **own home machines**; **25% take** on a **$1/lb** commodity; 500+ US cities, 150k+ "Laundry Pros"; **$10M seed (Headline, Mar 2022)**, ~$30M est. revenue (third-party, unverified), ~80 staff, no large later round → capital-efficient. **Thesis:** Poplin is **not a competitor — a different sport** (B2C convenience/commodity vs Blue Bee's B2B custody/specialty/contract); only overlap is the micro Airbnb/boutique-hotel edge it markets but does not truly serve (no SLAs/par/custody/specialty). Inbound signature verification (**`StripeEventVerifier`**, real local HMAC) is **always real**, independent of the provider. **Take-payment:** `POST /v1/orders/:id/payment-intent` (customer, own order) computes the amount **SERVER-side from the approved quote total incl. Ontario HST** (`createPaymentIntentSchema` carries NO amount — a client can't influence the charge), returns **only the `client_secret`**; re-attempt reuses the pending intent's idempotency key (same intent, no second charge); bounded by `PAYMENTS_MAX_ATTEMPTS`. **Charge-on-approval** (PRD A5.1 tight default), capture-later seam-ready (`PAYMENTS_CAPTURE_TIMING`). **The webhook** `POST /v1/webhooks/stripe` (`@Public`, **raw body** via `NestFactory({ rawBody:true })` in main.ts + the e2e factory; a SLASH route per the Inc-9 lesson): verify the raw-body signature → **400 on forged/unsigned/tampered**; then in ONE transaction **claim the event id in `ProcessedStripeEvent` (INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING — the Inc-5 pattern) + drive the state machine** so a row's existence == the event was fully applied (redelivery + concurrency are no-ops); post-commit it fires receipt/dunning/dispute notifications. **State machines, advanced ONLY by a webhook-confirmed event** (never the client's say-so): Payment PENDING→SUCCEEDED|FAILED→REFUNDED (illegal → guarded no-op); Invoice DRAFT/ISSUED→PAID|OVERDUE; Refund PENDING→SUCCEEDED (full **and** partial — refund ≤ captured via an **atomic optimistic reserve**, charge.refunded **reconciles to Stripe's authoritative total** + captures dashboard-initiated refunds); Dispute OPEN→UNDER_REVIEW→WON|LOST|CLOSED (recorded + surfaced + alerted, never adjudicated). Order **billing state** is mirrored onto `order.totals.paymentStatus` (PAID/FAILED/REFUNDED/PARTIALLY_REFUNDED) atomically in the webhook tx (the §5.1 fulfilment spine is NOT corrupted — charge-on-approval happens mid-flow; only a conservative DELIVERED→INVOICED advance). **Invoicing:** `POST /orders/:id/invoice` (owner/finance) generates Invoice + **InvoiceLine** rows from the quote (lines + the HST line), finalizes/sends + exercises the Stripe-Invoice outbound seam; the auto-receipt for a PaymentIntent success is the customer's PAID invoice. **Refunds/disputes/invoices are owner/finance-only** (`@Roles(OWNER, FINANCE)`); a customer pays only their own order (the proven `OrderAccessService` gate → 403 cross-account / 404 cross-org). **Receipts + dunning + dispute alerts reuse the Inc-8 engine** (`dispatchPayment` → matrix + preferences/quiet-hours + the idempotent outbox + lease-claimed retry/backoff): receipts/dunning → the owning customer, dispute alerts → org owner/finance (`resolveOrgStaffRecipients`); dunning is urgent (bypasses quiet hours). **Customer Pay surface** (`apps/customer`): real **`@stripe/stripe-react-native` PaymentSheet** (the card never touches our app/API — only the client secret crosses) behind a `StripeProvider`; a `Pay $X` CTA on the approved order → PaymentSheet → **polls `GET /orders/:id/payment` until the WEBHOOK confirms SUCCEEDED** (REST-is-truth, like tracking — never "paid" on the client's say-so) → shows the receipt; retry on failure; "processing" if the webhook hasn't landed. **Additive reversible migration `20260626120000_payments_inc12`**: ALTER Payment (Stripe linkage + currency + captured/refunded amounts + capture_method + attempts + idempotency_key, method default CARD), ALTER Invoice (order_id + number + stripe_invoice_id + issued/paid/due timestamps), NEW InvoiceLine/Refund/Dispute/`ProcessedStripeEvent` (append-only trigger, tamper-evident) + RefundStatus/DisputeStatus enums — **9 migrations + seed clean on a fresh DB**. **6-lens adversarial QC gate (parallel read-only subagents, refute-by-default): all 6 lenses verified; 3 findings fixed + fix-verified** — (HIGH) `generateInvoice`'s existing-check was outside the tx → a concurrent call/auto-receipt race hit the unique `number` constraint (500) → now catches P2002 → returns the existing invoice idempotently; (defense-in-depth) the payment/invoice read paths now scope by the gated order's `org_id`; (documented) the post-commit notification dispatch is best-effort (a crash in that window skips a receipt, recoverable — consistent with the Inc-8 geofence engine; Pure math (`eta-math.ts`) is fully unit-tested; the estimator wires it to PostGIS. **Recompute gating:** runs ONLY on a genuine last-known advance (the Lua-CAS monotonicity = the §10.4 "ping cluster" sampling), off the AUTHORITATIVE head position, for the vehicle's current active stop only (workable route + stop `EN_ROUTE`/`ARRIVED`, most-progressed — never pending/terminal); optional extra throttle `ETA_RECOMPUTE_MIN_INTERVAL_SEC` (default 0). Persisted on `RouteStop.eta`/`eta_low`/`eta_high`/`eta_computed_at` and emitted as the `eta` WS event. **Geofence** (reuses the ETA's distance — no extra query): `APPROACHING` fires within `GEOFENCE_APPROACHING_RADIUS_M` **OR** ETA ≤ `GEOFENCE_APPROACHING_ETA_SEC` (the §6.4 "800 m / 5 min", whichever first); `ARRIVED` within the tight `GEOFENCE_ARRIVED_RADIUS_M`. **Each type fires EXACTLY once per stop** — `@@unique([route_stop_id, type])` + `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` (Inc-5 idempotency) → jitter/re-entry/replay/**concurrent same-vehicle batches** never re-fire; emit only when a row was newly written. **`ARRIVED` is ADVISORY — does NOT transition the §5.4 stop** (driver stays authoritative, Inc 4 — test-locked). **Realtime scoping:** `publishEta`/`publishGeofence` → the customer's **STOP room** (stop-specific, so a co-routed customer on a DIFFERENT stop never sees this stop's ETA) + the **org fleet room**; never cross-customer, never cross-org. **REST first-paint:** `GET /orders/:id/tracking` returns `stop.{eta,etaLow,etaHigh,etaComputedAt}`; `GET /fleet/live` returns each vehicle's `nextStopEta`. **Degradation (§12.3):** the whole predictive path is wrapped in `TrackingPredictionService` — any estimator/geofence throw is logged + swallowed so it can NEVER fail `POST /locations:batch`; REST last-known + the status timeline stay source of truth (test-locked). **tx-scoping (Inc-4 lesson):** every server-internal query/write carries an explicit `org_id` (no scoped-extension reliance off-request); raw SQL fully parameterized; Redis keys org-namespaced. **Config-driven (Inc-3 lesson):** all `ETA_*` + `GEOFENCE_*` tunables in `tracking.config.ts`/`.env` (circuity 1.3, urban 30 km/h, floor/ceil 5/90, alpha 0.5, range 0.2, cap 14400 s, sample 5, min-move 15 m; approaching 800 m/300 s, arrived 100 m) — no literals. **As-built↔PRD reconciliation (recorded in `ARCHITECTURE.md`):** the PRD writes ETA "via Routes API" (§6.2); Inc 7 computes it **in-house behind the seam** — faithful to the PRD's own §11.2/§10.2 provider-abstraction intent (a Google Routes/Mapbox adapter swaps in later without touching callers, exactly like Inc-4's sequencer behind `RouteOptimizer`). **No notification delivery + intentionally NO stub sender** (also a §10.2 seam) — the durable `GeofenceEvent` row + the WS event ARE the triggers Increment 8 consumes. **Additive migration `20260624200000_eta_geofence_inc7`** (reversible): `RouteStop.+eta_low/+eta_high/+eta_computed_at` (nullable) + `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX GeofenceEvent_route_stop_id_type_key`; 6 migrations + seed clean on a fresh DB. **QC gate**: 6-lens adversarial review (background Workflow, 9 agents: no-stub/no-external-API · ETA correctness · geofence correctness · privacy/scoping · concurrency/tenancy/tx-scoping · degradation/integration) + per-finding refute-by-default skeptic → **3 raw → 1 refuted, 2 minors confirmed, 0 blockers/majors** (the other 4 lenses found nothing); **both minors FIXED + fix-verified** (geofence wire-type now an exhaustive `WIRE_TYPE` map so a future `DEPARTED` can't be mislabeled as `ARRIVED`; degradation catch logs `err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)`). No-stub sweep clean (real PostGIS distance + real math + real `GeofenceEvent` writes + real emit; no maps SDK/import/key; estimator bound to in-house impl; no fake notification sender). **Tests: 87 unit + 70 e2e green** (was 70 + 63; +17 unit `eta-math`, +7 e2e: ETA range on REST + emitted on customer & fleet + SHRINKS; approaching-once + arrived-once + idempotent under replay & concurrent batches; arrived does NOT transition the §5.4 stop; cross-org isolation; estimator/geofence throw never fails ingest + recovers; seam bound to in-house impl). **Live proof** (real OTP login → moving track): ETA `1905→1130→583→296→148 s` (monotone shrink), `APPROACHING`+`ARRIVED` each once, stop stayed `EN_ROUTE`, durable rows written, REST range present. `GET /v1/health` 200 (postgis 3.4.3 + timescaledb 2.26.4 + redis up). Real Socket.IO mounted on the Nest HTTP server with the **`@socket.io/redis-adapter`** (cross-instance fan-out) behind a thin `RealtimePublisher` abstraction (Ably/Pusher swap-in later, §10.2). **Two namespaces, authorized at the handshake and SERVER-BOUND (no client subscribe):** `WS /live/vehicle` (customer) — `auth.token` = the signed channel token; bound to exactly one vehicle room + its stop room. `WS /live/fleet` (OWNER/DISPATCHER/CSR) — `auth.token` = the access JWT (verified + revocation-checked + role-gated); bound to the caller-org fleet room (all org vehicles, org-scoped; different org gets nothing). **Signed channel token** (§6.2 privacy headline): `GET /v1/orders/:id/tracking` now returns `{ lastKnown, channelToken }` — a short-lived **dedicated-secret** HS256 JWT (`CHANNEL_TOKEN_SECRET`, never the auth JWT), `kind: 'vehicle-tracking'`, claims `{ org_id, vehicle_id, order_id, stop_id, sub, exp }`; the handshake verifies signature+expiry+kind **and re-checks LIVE stop state** (route PUBLISHED/IN_PROGRESS, stop non-terminal, route still points at the bound vehicle), so a token for a completed/failed/reassigned stop is useless even before `exp`. **Publish step:** ingestion emits a position to the vehicle + fleet rooms ONLY when the Lua-CAS advanced last-known, emitting the authoritative readback head gated on "this batch is still the head" — monotonic on the wire even under concurrent same-vehicle batches (no regression, no storm). **Lifecycle teardown (no leak after delivery):** force-disconnect on token `exp` (per-socket timer), stop terminal transition (`closeStopChannel`, after the §5.4 tx commits), stop reassignment to a different vehicle (`closeStopChannel('stop_reassigned')`), and shift end (`closeVehicleChannel`). **Graceful degradation (§12.3):** the WS is an optimization; the Inc-5 REST last-known + status timeline stay the source of truth; `publishPosition` no-ops if the gateway isn't bound. **WS contract documented in `ARCHITECTURE.md`** (OpenAPI doesn't cover WS): namespaces, auth, `position`/`channel:closed` events, error codes, lifecycle. **No DB migration** (additive code + 2 new env vars `CHANNEL_TOKEN_SECRET`/`CHANNEL_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS`; the 5 existing migrations re-verified clean on a fresh DB). New deps: `@nestjs/websockets`, `@nestjs/platform-socket.io`, `socket.io`, `@socket.io/redis-adapter` (+ `socket.io-client` dev). **QC gate**: 6-lens adversarial review (background Workflow, 15 agents) + per-finding skeptic → 9 raw → **2 majors + 4 minors confirmed; both majors + 2 minors FIXED + fix-verified, 3 minors documented/justified** (5 skeptics hit transient 529s → re-verified by hand). Majors fixed: WS publish ordering under concurrent same-vehicle batches (emit readback head gated on still-the-head); reassign-to-different-vehicle privacy leak (teardown on reassign). Minors fixed: channel token `auth.token`-only (no token in URL); Redis pub/sub closed on shutdown. No-stub sweep clean (real Socket.IO + Redis adapter + signed JWT; dedicated channel-token secret). **Tests: 70 unit + 63 e2e green** (incl. real `socket.io-client` WS e2e: fleet stream + cross-org isolation, customer one-vehicle scoping, token security expired/forged/cross-vehicle, stop-complete + shift-end + reassign teardown, publish monotonicity, degradation). Live WS proof (real client) showed fleet+customer streams, channel-token rejected on `/live/fleet`, and `channel:closed{stop_completed}` + reconnect `stop_not_active`. Node 20.20.2. → packages/shared (WS contract), apps/api (realtime module + gateway/adapter/token/publisher + tests + e2e; ingestion publish; tracking-read mint; logistics teardown; main.ts adapter; auth.config secret)
- 19:25 IST · **[BLUE BEE] Live tracking — location ingestion + storage shipped (Increment 5 — BUILD-PLAN item 7, ingestion slice).** New `tracking` module on the Increment-4 logistics foundation; the **ingestion + storage + scoped-read data path only** — STOPS BEFORE the realtime layer (no Socket.IO/WS channels, no signed customer channel token, no server-side ETA, no geofence/GeofenceEvent, no notifications — Increment 6; those models stay modelled, unused). **`GET /fleet/live`** (OWNER/DISPATCH/CSR) — all org vehicles' last-known; **`GET /orders/:id/tracking`** (owning customer + staff) — last-known of the single vehicle on the order's active stop. **Shift-gated (privacy, §6.2/§12.2):** pings accepted ONLY for an ACTIVE shift — ended/scheduled → `409 shift_not_active`, missing → `404 shift_not_found`, vehicle≠shift's → `422 vehicle_shift_mismatch`; never stored outside an active shift. **Scoped:** shift/driver/vehicle loaded via the scoped Prisma client (org boundary); a DRIVER may post ONLY for their own shift (`403 not_own_shift`), OWNER for any org shift. **Robust ingestion (§6.2):** in-batch dedup by `(vehicleId, recordedAt)` (last-wins, sorted), DB-level dedup via `INSERT … ON CONFLICT (vehicle_id, recorded_at) DO NOTHING` (replays append 0 rows), out-of-order tolerant — **`recorded_at` is authoritative for "latest"**, malformed coords/timestamps → `400`. **Redis last-known (real, hot):** per-org GEO sorted set `bb:fleetgeo:{org}` (GEOADD → nearest-vehicle ready for Inc 6) + per-vehicle hash, advanced by an **atomic Lua CAS** that only moves forward on a strictly-newer `recorded_at` (a late/older ping never regresses last-known, even under concurrent bursts). **Timescale history (real):** every accepted ping appended to the `GpsPing` hypertable via one parameterized multi-row `INSERT` (PostGIS `ST_MakePoint`) — the replay/audit record. **Write order keeps last-known fresh under throttled history (§10.4):** Redis CAS first, then history append; a failed append is reconciled on retry (CAS no-ops + ON CONFLICT idempotent), and `fleet/live` falls back to the latest hypertable row when Redis is cold. **As-built↔PRD drift reconciled** (additive migration `20260624190000_tracking_inc5`): ONE change — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX GpsPing_vehicle_id_recorded_at_key` on the existing hypertable (covers the partition col `recorded_at`, Timescale-legal), mirrored as `@@unique([vehicleId, recordedAt])`; no new tables/columns; reversible. **tx-scoping (Increment-4 lesson):** the ingestion path uses no Prisma `$transaction` — every raw-SQL ping row and fallback read carries an **explicit `org_id`** (no scoped-extension reliance), Redis keys are org-namespaced. **QC gate**: 6-lens adversarial review (no-stub · privacy/shift-gating · ingestion correctness · dual-store integrity · tx-scoping · RBAC/concurrency) as a background Workflow with a per-finding skeptic → **0 findings (agents affirmatively validated every critical path); nothing to fix**. No-stub sweep clean (both stores real + exercised). **Tests: 59 unit + 55 e2e green** (incl. shift-gating, out-of-order/dedup/replay, concurrent batches, dual-store, fleet/live fallback, customer single-vehicle privacy, RBAC/cross-org). Live curl proved ingest→Redis(`HGETALL`/`GEOPOS`/`GEOSEARCH`)+Timescale(18 pings/3 vehicles/real hypertable chunk); all 5 migrations + seed clean on a fresh DB; `GET /v1/health` 200. Local infra note: a host `redis-server` on 127.0.0.1:6379 shadows the docker `bluebee-redis` container — app/e2e/proof all use the host one consistently (`docker exec bluebee-redis redis-cli` reads the wrong empty instance; use `redis-cli -h 127.0.0.1`). the route-stop pickup is the primary path. **In-house sequencer** (`POST /routes/:id/optimize`): real greedy nearest-neighbour over **PostGIS `ST_Distance`** geography from a depot, respecting stop time windows, behind a `RouteOptimizer` seam so Google/Onfleet/Routific swap in later — no external map API this increment, no hand-rolled haversine. **Manifest** generation per stop/route (expected items/orders — the pickup check-list, §6.3/§9.5). **As-built↔PRD drift reconciled** (additive migration `20260624180000_logistics_inc4`): added §9.5 `Manifest` model (Increment 1 deferred it), `RouteStop.exception_reason`, `Route.start_geo` (depot). Prisma schema for the §9 Phase-1 slice (28 models + `Service`; every row has `id`/`org_id`/`created_at`/`updated_at`/`created_by`; 5 PostGIS `geography(Point,4326)` columns; `GpsPing` composite PK). Seed inserts one honest demo specialty order (org→owner→residential account→site w/ real PostGIS point→service→order→line→tag→item→inspection→photo→quote + 3 custody events), walked through a validated §5.1 path (`REQUESTED→…→QUOTED`). **Infra change (flagged):** swapped `infra/docker-compose.yml` Postgres from `timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16` to a custom `infra/Dockerfile.postgres` (`postgis/postgis:16` + TimescaleDB apt pkg, stock postgres entrypoint) — the `-ha` image makes Prisma fail `P1010` on every connection; same locked stack (PG16+PostGIS+Timescale), do **not** revert. Verified via a 4-reviewer adversarial pass (0 blockers/majors).
